Continuing work from thread T-019b275d-95a8-747c-8296-9ac1df203a6d. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@docs/feature/csv-editor.md @src/csv/model.rs @src/csv/navigation.rs @src/csv/render.rs @src/update/csv.rs @src/view/mod.rs @src/messages.rs @src/runtime/app.rs @src/update/mod.rs @src/csv/parser.rs

I just completed Phase 1 of the CSV Viewer feature, which is now fully working with:
- Grid rendering with row/column headers
- Cell selection via mouse click and keyboard navigation
- Arrow keys, Tab/Shift+Tab, Home/End, Page Up/Down, Cmd+Home/End
- Mouse wheel scrolling (vertical and horizontal)
- Delimiter detection from file extension or content
- Command palette integration via "Toggle CSV View"

I also updated the docs (CHANGELOG.md, README.md, docs/feature/csv-editor.md) to reflect Phase 1 completion.

Phase 2 is Cell Editing. The design doc at docs/feature/csv-editor.md (lines 2002-2016) specifies:
- CellEditState to track edit buffer, cursor position, original value
- Cell editor rendering with text cursor inside cell
- Keyboard input while editing (chars, backspace, arrows within cell)
- Enter confirms edit, Escape cancels
- Document sync: rebuild CSV line with proper escaping, update document buffer
- Undo/redo integration

New messages needed for Phase 2:
- CsvMsg::StartEditing, StartEditingWithChar(char), ConfirmEdit, CancelEdit
- CsvMsg::EditInsertChar(char), EditDeleteBackward, EditCursorLeft, EditCursorRight

Key implementation details:
- Add editing: Option<CellEditState> field to CsvState
- CellEditState contains: position (CellPosition), buffer (String), cursor (usize), original (String)
- When edit confirmed, reconstruct entire line with proper CSV escaping using detected delimiter
- Handle quoting for values containing delimiter or newlines
- Click elsewhere should confirm current edit

The CSV module structure is:
- src/csv/mod.rs - module exports
- src/csv/model.rs - CsvState, CsvData, CellPosition, Delimiter
- src/csv/parser.rs - parsing with csv crate
- src/csv/render.rs - CsvRenderLayout, column_to_letters, pixel_to_csv_cell
- src/csv/navigation.rs - cell navigation methods on CsvState
- src/csv/viewport.rs - CsvViewport for scrolling

Rendering happens in src/view/mod.rs in render_csv_grid() function.
Update routing is in src/update/csv.rs and src/update/mod.rs (intercepts EditorMsg when in CSV mode).
Mouse/keyboard events are handled in src/runtime/app.rs.

Test with "make csv" which opens a 10k row sample file.

continue with phase 2

InconsistentColumns { expected: usize, found: usize, row: usize },\n519:     /// Truncated due to size limit\n520:     Truncated { max_rows: usize },\n521:     /// Invalid UTF-8 replaced\n522:     InvalidUtf8 { row: usize },\n523: }\n524: \n525: /// Parse CSV from document buffer\n526: pub fn parse_csv(\n527:     source: &str,\n528:     delimiter: u8,\n529:     max_rows: Option<usize>,\n530: ) Vec::new();\n540:     \n541:     for (row_idx, result) in reader.records().enumerate() {\n542:         // Check row limit\n543:         if let Some(max) = max_rows {\n544:             if row_idx >= col_count,\n559:                         row: row_idx,\n560:                     });\n561:                 }\n562:                 max_cols = max_cols.max(col_count);\n563:                 \n564: Log but continue (flexible parsing)\n574:                 tracing::warn!(\"CSV parse error at row {}: {}\", row_idx, e);\n575:                 rows.push(String::new());\n576:             }\n577:         }\n578:     }\n579:     \n580:     Ok(ParseResult {\n581:         data: CsvData {\n582:             rows,\n583:             field_delimiter: CsvData::FIELD_DELIMITER,\n584:             column_count: delimiter by sampling first few lines\n596: pub fn detect_delimiter(source: &str) -> u8 {\n597:     const CANDIDATES: &[u8] = &[b',', b'\\t', b';', b'|'];\n598:     const SAMPLE_LINES: usize = 5;\n599:     \n600:     let lines: Vec<&str> = source.lines().take(SAMPLE_LINES).collect();\n601:     if lines.is_empty() {\n602:         return b','; // Default\n603:     }\n604:     \n605:     let mut scores: Vec<(u8, f64)> = CANDIDATES\n606:         .iter()\n607:         .map(|&delim| {\n608:             let counts: Vec<usize> = lines\n609:                 .iter()\n610:                 .map(|line| line.bytes().filter(|&b| b == delim).count())\n611:                 .collect();\n612:             \n613:             // Score: consistency (low variance) + presence (count > 0)\n614:             let mean = counts.iter().sum::<usize>() as f64 / counts.len() as f64;\n615:             if mean == 0.0 {\n616:                 return (delim, 0.0);\n617:             }\n618:             \n619:             let variance = counts.iter()\n620:                 .map(|&c| (c as f64 - mean).powi(2))\n621:                 .sum::<f64>() / counts.len() as f64;\n622:             \n623:             // Higher score = better (high count, low variance)\n624:             let consistency = if variance == 0.0 { 1.0 } else { 1.0 / (1.0 + variance) };\n625:             (delim, mean * consistency)\n626:         })\n627:         .collect();\n628:     \n629:     scores.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.partial_cmp(&a.1).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));\n630:     \n631:     scores.first().map(|(d, _)| *d).unwrap_or(b',')\n632: }\n633: ```\n634: \n635: ### 5.3 Incremental Re-parse (Phase 2+)\n636: \n637: For editing, we need to detect which rows changed:\n638: \n639: ```rust\n640: /// Track what   "content": "1: //! CSV viewport calculations\n2: //!\n3: //! Tracks which portion of the CSV grid is visible.\n4: \n5: /// Viewport state for CSV grid\n6: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n7: pub struct CsvViewport {\n8:     /// First visible row (0-indexed)\n9:     pub top_row: usize,\n10:     /// First visible column (0-indexed)\n11:     pub left_col: usize,\n12:     /// Number of rows that fit in the viewport\n13:     pub visible_rows: usize,\n14:     /// Approximate number of columns visible (depends on column widths)\n15:     pub visible_cols: usize,\n16: }\n17: \n18: impl CsvViewport {\n19:     /// Create a new viewport with given dimensions\n20:     pub fn new(visible_rows: usize, visible_cols: usize) -> Self {\n21:         Self {\n22:             top_row: 0,\n23:             left_col: 0,\n24:             visible_rows,\n25:             visible_cols,\n26:         }\n27:     }\n28: \n29:     /// Ensure a cell is visible, scrolling if necessary\n30:     pub fn ensure_visible(&mut self, row: usize, col: usize, total_rows: usize, total_cols: usize) {\n31:         // Vertical scrolling\n32:         if row < self.top_row {\n33:             self.top_row = row;\n34:         } else if row >= self.top_row + self.visible_rows && self.visible_rows > 0 {\n35:             self.top_row = row.saturating_sub(self.visible_rows - 1);\n36:         }\n37: \n38:         // Horizontal scrolling\n39:         if col < self.left_col {\n40:             self.left_col = col;\n41:         } else if col >= self.left_col + self.visible_cols && self.visible_cols > 0 {\n42:             self.left_col = col.saturating_sub(self.visible_cols - 1);\n43:         }\n44: \n45:         // Clamp to valid range\n46:         let max_top = total_rows.saturating_sub(self.visible_rows);\n47:         let max_left = total_cols.saturating_sub(self.visible_cols);\n48:         self.top_row = self.top_row.min(max_top);\n49:         self.left_col = self.left_col.min(max_left);\n50:     }\n51: \n52:     /// Check if a row is visible\n53:     pub fn is_row_visible(&self, row: usize) -> bool {\n54:         row >= self.top_row && row < self.top_row + self.visible_rows\n55:     }\n56: \n57:     /// Check if a column is visible\n58:     pub fn is_col_visible(&self, col: usize) -> bool {\n59:         col >= self.left_col && col < self.left_col + self.visible_cols\n60:     }\n61: }\n62: \n63: #[cfg(test)]\n64: mod tests {\n65:     use super::*;\n66: \n67:     #[test]\n68:     fn test_viewport_ensure_visible_scroll_down() {\n69:         let mut vp = CsvViewport::new(10, 5);\n70:         vp.ensure_visible(15, 0, 100, 20);\n71:         assert_eq!(vp.top_row, 6); // 15 - {\n+    /// Position of the cell being edited\n+    pub position: CellPosition,\n+    /// Current edit buffer content\n+    pub buffer: String,\n+    /// Cursor position within buffer (byte offset)\n+    pub cursor: usize,\n+    /// Original value before editing (for cancel/undo)\n+    pub original: String,\n+}\n+\n+impl CellEditState {\n+    /// Create new edit state for a cell\n+    pub fn new(position: CellPosition, value: String) -> Self {\n+        let cursor = value.len();\n+        Self {\n+            position,\n+            buffer: value.clone(),\n+            cursor,\n+            original: value,\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Create new edit state starting with a character (replaces content)\n+    pub fn with_char(position: CellPosition, original: String, ch: char) -> Self {\n+        Self {\n+            position,\n+            buffer: ch.to_string(),\n+            cursor: ch.len_utf8(),\n+            original,\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Insert character at cursor position\n+    pub fn insert_char(&mut self, ch: char) {\n+        self.buffer.insert(self.cursor, ch);\n+        self.cursor += ch.len_utf8();\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Delete character before cursor (backspace)\n+    pub fn delete_backward(&mut self) {\n+        if self.cursor > 0 {\n+            let prev_boundary = self.buffer[..self.cursor]\n+                .char_indices()\n+                .last()\n+                .map(|(i, _)| i)\n+                .unwrap_or(0);\n+            self.buffer.remove(prev_boundary);\n+            self.cursor = prev_boundary;\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Delete character at cursor (delete)\n+    pub fn delete_forward(&mut self) {\n+        if self.cursor < self.buffer.len() {\n+            self.buffer.remove(self.cursor);\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move cursor left\n+    pub fn cursor_left(&mut self) {\n+        if self.cursor > 0 {\n+            self.cursor = self.buffer[..self.cursor]\n+                .char_indices()\n+                .last()\n+                .map(|(i, _)| i)\n+                .unwrap_or(0);\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move cursor right\n+    pub fn cursor_right(&mut self) {\n+        if self.cursor < self.buffer.len() {\n+            if let Some((i, ch)) = self.buffer[self.cursor..].char_indices().next() {\n+                self.cursor += ch.len_utf8();\n+            }\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move cursor to start\n+    pub fn cursor_home(&mut self) {\n+        self.cursor = 0;\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move cursor to end\n+    pub fn cursor_end(&mut self) {\n+        self.cursor = self.buffer.len();\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Check if content changed from original\n+    pub fn is_modified(&self) -> bool {\n+        self.buffer != self.original\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Get cursor position in characters (for rendering)\n+    pub fn cursor_char_position(&self) -> usize {\n+        self.buffer[..self.cursor].chars().count()\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+/// Represents a completed cell edit for sync/undo\n+#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n+pub struct CellEdit {\n+    pub position: CellPosition,\n+    pub old_value: String,\n+    pub new_value: String,\n+}\n+\n /// Memory-efficient CSV data storage\n ///\n /// Instead of storing `Vec<Vec<String>>` which has significant overhead,\n /// each row is stored as a single string with cells delimited by CELL_DELIMITER (0xFA).\n```",

keys to the cell editor instead of the normal editor.\n+#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n+fn handle_csv_edit_key(\n+    model: &mut AppModel,\n+    key: Key,\n+    ctrl: bool,\n+    _shift: bool,\n+    alt: bool,\n+    logo: bool,\n+) -> Option<Cmd> {\n+    match key {\n+        // Escape: cancel edit\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::Escape) => update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::CancelEdit)),\n+\n+        // Enter: confirm edit and move down\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::Enter) => {\n+            let cmd = update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit));\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::MoveDown));\n+            cmd\n+        }\n+\n+        // Tab: confirm edit and move to next cell\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::Tab) => {\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit));\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::NextCell))\n+        }\n+\n+        // Arrow Left/Right: move cursor within cell\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowLeft) if !alt && !ctrl && !logo => {\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::EditCursorLeft))\n+        }\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowRight) if !alt && !ctrl && !logo => {\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::EditCursorRight))\n+        }\n+\n+        // Arrow Up/Down: confirm edit and navigate\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowUp) => {\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit));\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::MoveUp))\n+        }\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowDown) => {\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit));\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::MoveDown))\n+        }\n+\n+        // Home/End: move cursor to start/end\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::Home) => update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::EditCursorHome)),\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::End) => update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::EditCursorEnd)),\n+\n+        // Backspace: delete backward\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::Backspace) => update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::EditDeleteBackward)),\n+\n+        // Delete: delete forward\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::Delete) => update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::EditDeleteForward)),\n+\n+        // Space\n+        Key::Named(NamedKey::Space) if !(ctrl || logo) => {\n+            update(model, Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::EditInsertChar(' ')))\n+        }\n+\n+        // Character input\n+        Key::Character(ref s) if !(ctrl || logo) => {\n+            let mut cmd = None;\n+            for ch in s.chars() {\n+                cmd = update(model,   "content": "1: # CSV Viewer/Editor Mode\n2: \n3: A spreadsheet-like view for tabular data files (CSV, TSV, PSV) with cell editing, horizontal scrolling, and synchronization with the underlying text buffer.\n4: \n5: > **Status:** Phase 1 Complete ✅ (Read-only viewer with full navigation)\n6: > **Inspired by:** [Tablecruncher](https://github.com/Tablecruncher/tablecruncher), TablePlus\n7: > **Target:** ~~Phase 1 (Read-only)~~ → Phase 2 (Cell Editing) → Phase 3 (Advanced Features)\n8: \n9: ---\n10: \n11: ## Phase 1 Implementation Summary\n12: \n13: Phase 1 delivers a fully functional read-only CSV viewer with:\n14: \n15: - **Grid rendering** with row numbers (1, 2, 3...) and column headers (A, B, C...)\n16: - **Cell selection** via mouse click or keyboard navigation\n17: - **Keyboard navigation**: Arrow keys, Tab/Shift+Tab, Home/End, Page Up/Down, Cmd+Home/End\n18: - **Mouse wheel scrolling** for both vertical and horizontal navigation\n19: - **Delimiter detection** from file extension (.csv, .tsv, .psv) or content sniffing\n20: - **Column width auto-calculation** based on content (sampled from first 100 rows)\n21: - **Theme integration** with configurable colors for headers, grid lines, selection\n22: - **Command palette integration** via \"Toggle CSV View\" command\n23: - **Large file support** tested with 10,000+ row files\n24: \n25: **Test command:** `make csv` opens a 10,001-line sample CSV file for testing.\n26: \n27: ---\n28: \n29: ## Design Decisions\n30: \n31: These decisions were resolved during planning:\n32: \n33: | Decision | Resolution |\n34: |----------|------------|\n35: | **State preservation** | Discard state on toggle. Re-parse on each toggle. Simple, stateless approach. |\n36: | **Auto-enable** | No auto-enable. Manual toggle via command palette (\"Toggle CSV View\"). Users can bind their own shortcut. |\n37: | **Size limits** | No hard limits. Implementation should handle large files efficiently. Defer warnings if needed later. |\n38: | **Internal delimiter (0xFA)** | Accepted risk. Re-assess if collisions become an issue in practice. |\n39: \n40: ---\n41: \n42: ## Table of Contents\n43: \n44: 1. [Overview](#1-overview)\n45: 2. [User Experience](#2-user-experience)\n46: 3. [Architecture](#3-architecture)\n47: 4. [Data Model](#4-data-model)\n48: 5. [Parsing Strategy](#5-parsing-strategy)\n49: 6. [Rendering](#6-rendering)\n50: 7. [Cell Navigation & Selection](#7-cell-navigation--selection)\n51: 8. [Cell Editing (Phase 2)](#8-cell-editing-phase-2)\n52: 9. [Synchronization](#9-synchronization)\n53: 10. [Error Handling](#10-error-handling)\n54: 11. [Performance & Benchmarks](#11-performance--benchmarks)\n55: 12. [Testing Strategy](#12-testing-strategy)\n56: 13. [Implementation Phases](#13-implementation-phases)\n57: 14. [Message Types](#14-message-types)\n58: 15. [Commands](#15-commands)\n59: 16.   "content": "1: # Changelog\n2: \n3: All notable changes to rust-editor are documented in this file.\n4: \n5: ---\n6: \n7: ## v0.3.5 - 2025-12-16 (Latest)\n8: \n9: ### Added - CSV Viewer Mode (Phase 1)\n10: \n11: Spreadsheet-like view for CSV, TSV, and PSV files with full navigation support:\n12: \n13: **Core Features:**\n14: - **Grid rendering** with row numbers (1, 2, 3...) and column headers (A, B, C...)\n15: - **Cell selection** via mouse click with proper hit-testing\n16: - **Delimiter detection** from file extension or content sniffing\n17: - **Column width auto-calculation** based on content (sampled from first 100 rows)\n18: - **Theme integration** with configurable colors for headers, grid lines, selection\n19: - **Memory-efficient storage** using delimited strings (Tablecruncher pattern)\n20: \n21: **Navigation:**\n22: - Arrow keys move cell selection\n23: - Tab/Shift+Tab for next/previous cell with row wrapping\n24: - Home/End for first/last column in row\n25: - Cmd+Home/End for first/last cell in document\n26: - Page Up/Down jumps by viewport height\n27: - Mouse wheel scrolling (vertical and horizontal)\n28: - Click to select cell\n29: \n30: **Integration:**\n31: - Command palette: \"Toggle CSV View\" command\n32: - Escape exits CSV mode\n33: - Status bar shows CSV mode indicator\n34: - Works with split views\n35: \n36: **New files:**\n37: - `src/csv/` module with `mod.rs`, `model.rs`, `parser.rs`, `render.rs`, `navigation.rs`, `viewport.rs`\n38: - `samples/large_data.csv` - 10,001-line test file\n39: - `make csv` target for testing\n40: \n41: **Messages added:**\n42: - `CsvMsg` enum with Toggle, Move*, NextCell, PrevCell, FirstCell, LastCell, RowStart, RowEnd, PageUp, PageDown, Exit, SelectCell, ScrollVertical, ScrollHorizontal\n43: \n44: **Documentation:** See [docs/feature/csv-editor.md](feature/csv-editor.md)\n45: \n46: ---\n47: \n48: ## v0.3.4 - 2025-12-16\n49: \n50: ### Fixed - HiDPI Display Switching\n[... omitted lines 51 to 1134 ...]",

  "diff": "```diff\nIndex: ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\n===================================================================\n--- ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\toriginal\n+++ ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\tmodified\n@@ -3,10 +3,43 @@\n All notable changes to rust-editor are documented in this file.\n \n ---\n \n-## v0.3.5 - 2025-12-16 (Latest)\n+## v0.3.6 - 2025-12-16 (Latest)\n \n+### Added - CSV Cell Editing (Phase 2)\n+\n+Full cell editing support for CSV mode with document synchronization:\n+\n+**Editing:**\n+- **Enter or typing** starts editing the selected cell\n+- **Typing replaces** cell content when starting with a character\n+- **Edit cursor** navigation with Left/Right arrows, Home/End\n+- **Backspace/Delete** for character deletion\n+- **Enter confirms** edit and moves to next row\n+- **Tab confirms** edit and moves to next cell\n+- **Escape cancels** edit, restoring original value\n+\n+**Document Sync:**\n+- Edits update the underlying text buffer in real-time\n+- Proper CSV escaping for values with delimiters, quotes, or newlines\n+- Quoted fields are handled correctly (embedded commas don't break parsing)\n+- File becomes \"modified\" after edits, triggers save prompt\n+\n+**New types:**\n+- `CellEditState` - tracks edit buffer, cursor position, original value\n+- `CellEdit` - represents a completed edit for sync/undo\n+\n+**New messages:**\n+- `CsvMsg::StartEditing`, `StartEditingWithChar(char)`\n+- `CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit`, `CancelEdit`\n+- `CsvMsg::EditInsertChar`, `EditDeleteBackward`, `EditDeleteForward`\n+- `CsvMsg::EditCursorLeft`, `EditCursorRight`, `EditCursorHome`, `EditCursorEnd`\n+\n+---\n+\n+## v0.3.5 - 2025-12-16\n+\n ### Added - CSV Viewer Mode (Phase 1)\n \n Spreadsheet-like view for CSV, TSV, and PSV files with full navigation support:\n \n```",

## Assistant

All tests pass (189 library tests + 516 integration tests = 705 tests). Phase 2 is complete.

### Summary

I've implemented **Phase 2 (Cell Editing)** for the CSV viewer with the following changes:

#### New Types & Structures
- **`CellEditState`** ([src/csv/model.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/csv/model.rs#L54-L150)) - Tracks edit buffer, cursor position, and original value
- **`CellEdit`** ([src/csv/model.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/csv/model.rs#L152-L158)) - Represents a completed edit operation for sync

#### CsvState Editing Methods
- `start_editing()`, `start_editing_with_char(ch)`
- `confirm_edit()`, `cancel_edit()`
- `edit_insert_char()`, `edit_delete_backward()`, `edit_delete_forward()`
- `edit_cursor_left()`, `edit_cursor_right()`, `edit_cursor_home()`, `edit_cursor_end()`

#### New Messages
- `CsvMsg::StartEditing`, `StartEditingWithChar(char)`
- `CsvMsg::ConfirmEdit`, `CancelEdit`
- `CsvMsg::EditInsertChar`, `EditDeleteBackward`, `EditDeleteForward`
- `CsvMsg::EditCursorLeft/Right/Home/End`

#### Keyboard Input Routing
- [src/runtime/input.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/runtime/input.rs#L247-L330) - New `handle_csv_edit_key()` routes all input to cell editor when editing

#### Document Synchronization
- [src/update/csv.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/update/csv.rs#L390-L485) - `sync_cell_edit_to_document()` updates the underlying buffer with proper CSV escaping

#### Rendering
- [src/view/mod.rs](file://~/code/rust-editor/src/view/mod.rs#L936-L993) - `render_csv_cell_editor()` draws the edit overlay with cursor

#### Usage
1. Open a CSV file and toggle CSV mode (Command Palette → "Toggle CSV View")
2. Navigate to a cell with arrow keys
3. Press **Enter** to edit, or just **start typing** to replace content
4. Use **Left/Right/Home/End** to move cursor within cell
5. **Enter** confirms and moves down, **Tab** confirms and moves right
6. **Escape** cancels and restores original value
7. Edits are synced to the document buffer with proper CSV escaping

2. Make the update logic smarter to handle `InsertNewline` correctly when already editing

## Assistant

All tests pass. Now the behavior is:

**When editing a cell:**
- **Left/Right arrows** → move cursor within the cell
- **Up arrow** → confirm edit and move up to previous row
- **Down arrow** → confirm edit and move down to next row
- **Enter** → confirm edit and move down
- **Home/End** → move cursor to start/end within the cell
- **Backspace/Delete** → delete characters in the cell
- **Escape** → cancel edit

**When not editing:**
- All navigation keys work as before (move cell selection)
- **Enter** or **typing** → starts editing
